Defaults to "not confirmed" unless it can reproduce the defect itself. Used by the tectora-review skill to filter false positives before findings reach the user; not for general reviewing. ([jakubkodajek/Tectora](https://github.com/jakubkodajek/Tectora/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/tectora-finding-verifier.agent.md) · 2 copies)

Also fixes verified review findings. Use after the plan and failing tests exist, as step 3 of the tectora-feature skill. Writes findings to the shared review board. Use for "check the calculations", "is the roof math right", "verify areas", or as part of the tectora-review skill. Read-only on the source tree. Use before any non-trivial change, or as step 1 of the tectora-feature skill.
